I'll solve this step by step.

## Problem Understanding

1. We have t chats, each with n users and m messages
2. Some messages have unknown senders (marked as "?:")
3. We need to assign senders to unknown messages such that:
   - No two consecutive messages have the same sender
   - No sender mentions themselves in their message (the username appears as a word in the text)
4. If impossible, output "Impossible"

## Step-by-Step Solution

### Step 1: Parse the input and extract key information
- Users list
- Messages with known or unknown senders
- For each message, identify if it has a known sender or is unknown

### Step 2: Check constraints
- For messages with known senders, verify they don't mention themselves
- Check that consecutive messages don't have the same sender

### Step 3: Solve for unknown messages
- Use backtracking or greedy approach to assign senders
- For each unknown message, try all possible users that don't violate constraints

### Step 4: Implementation strategy
- First validate known messages (no self-mention and no consecutive same sender)
- Then solve for unknown messages using a greedy approach or backtracking
- For each unknown message, try users that don't mention themselves and don't match previous message's sender

Let me implement this:

```python
def get_words(text):
    """Extract words from text (sequences of letters and digits)"""
    words = []
    i = 0
    while i < len(text):
        if text[i].isalnum():
            word = ""
            while i < len(text) and text[i].isalnum():
                word += text[i]
                i += 1
            words.append(word)
        else:
            i += 1
    return words

def does_mention(text, username):
    """Check if username appears as a word in text"""
    words = get_words(text)
    return username in words

def can_assign_sender(current_sender, next_sender, text, users):
    """Check if we can assign next_sender to a message"""
    # Check if next_sender mentions himself
    if does_mention(text, next_sender):
        return False
    
    # Check if next_sender is different from current_sender
    if next_sender == current_sender:
        return False
    
    return True

def solve_chat(users, messages):
    # Parse messages
    assigned = []
    for message in messages:
        parts = message.split(':', 1)
        sender = parts[0]
        text = parts[1]
        
        if sender == "?":
            assigned.append(None)
        else:
            assigned.append(sender)
    
    # First, validate known messages
    for i in range(len(messages)):
        if assigned[i] is not None:
            # Check if known sender mentions himself
            if does_mention(messages[i].split(':', 1)[1], assigned[i]):
                return "Impossible"
    
    # Check consecutive messages in known cases
    for i in range(1, len(messages)):
        if assigned[i] is not None and assigned[i-1] is not None:
            if assigned[i] == assigned[i-1]:
                return "Impossible"
    
    # Now solve for unknown messages
    result = []
    current_sender = None
    
    for i in range(len(messages)):
        if assigned[i] is not None:
            result.append(f"{assigned[i]}:{messages[i].split(':', 1)[1]}")
            current_sender = assigned[i]
        else:
            # Try to find a valid sender for unknown message
            found = False
            for user in users:
                # Check if this user would mention themselves
                if does_mention(messages[i].split(':', 1)[1], user):
                    continue
                
                # Check if this user is different from previous message's sender
                if current_sender is not None and user == current_sender:
                    continue
                
                # This user works
                result.append(f"{user}:{messages[i].split(':', 1)[1]}")
                current_sender = user
                found = True
                break
            
            if not found:
                return "Impossible"
    
    return result

def main():
    t = int(input())
    
    for _ in range(t):
        n = int(input())
        users = input().split()
        
        m = int(input())
        messages = []
        for _ in range(m):
            messages.append(input())
        
        result = solve_chat(users, messages)
        
        if result == "Impossible":
            print("Impossible")
        else:
            for msg in result:
                print(msg)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```
